Users abandon when they can't predict what's coming. | Replace every content-area spinner with a **skeleton screen** that mirrors the final layout — same number of rows, same approximate widths, same image blocks. Spinner is reserved only for *sub-200ms* operations where a skeleton would flash. | Job list, course catalog, profile view, feed, application list, notification center, admin tables, institutional analytics. | Every content surface in §UXC-004 through §UXC-028 has a defined skeleton state. Zero spinners in content areas. Skeleton appears <100ms, disappears <50ms after data arrives. | PROGRESSIVE_UX_AND_FEEDBACK.md ?1; DESIGN_SYSTEM.md ?7.7; SCREEN_REGISTRY.md ?0 | conflict | Skeleton <100ms and removal <50ms conflict with target 150ms delay, >=300ms hold and six-row cap. | Seven reviewed UX target files only; not a whole-repository absence claim | OPEN-50; review relevant OPEN-UX decisions; no source pattern approved/rejected here |
| M-02 | The 100ms Rule | 4639 | 4645 | User clicks "Apply" or "Submit" or "Save." Nothing happens for 400ms. They click again. Now the form submits twice, or the button state is ambiguous. | Double-submissions, duplicate records, user anxiety, and support tickets. | Every button that triggers a mutation must give **visual feedback within 100ms** (Miller's response-time threshold) — a pressed state, a spinner *inside the button*, or an immediate state change — then **disable itself** until the response returns. Never rely on the round-trip to provide feedback. | Apply, Submit, Save, Post, Publish, Enroll, Purchase, Send, Follow, Connect, Report, Refund, Approve, Reject. | `<Button>` component has a mandatory `pending` prop. Lint rule blocks mutation buttons without a pending state. 100ms verified via synthetic click test. | PROGRESSIVE_UX_AND_FEEDBACK.md ?1, ?6.1; DESIGN_SYSTEM.md ?7.1 | conflict | Source disables mutation and requires pending/100ms checks; target keeps control enabled and ignores repeats. | Seven reviewed UX target files only; not a whole-repository absence claim | OPEN-50; review relevant OPEN-UX decisions; no source pattern approved/rejected here |
| M-03 | Layout Shift | 4649 | 4655 | User goes to click "Apply," but a late-loading banner pushes the button down 40px, and their click lands on "Save Job" or an ad. They didn't get what they clicked. | Frustration, accidental actions, ad-click fraud (in the video's example), broken trust. | **Reserve space** for every late-loading element. Banners, images, video players, embedded content, and third-party widgets must declare their dimensions in layout before content arrives. Use `aspect-ratio`, `min-height`, or explicit skeleton heights. | Job detail (salary widget, company logo), course player (video frame), profile (avatar, banner), feed (media, link previews), institutional dashboard (charts). | Cumulative Layout Shift (CLS) <0.1 on every route class (§35.2). Zero post-click element displacement in E2E tests. | PROGRESSIVE_UX_AND_FEEDBACK.md ?1; NAVIGATION_ARCHITECTURE.md ?13 | partial | Universal late-element dimensions and zero post-click displacement test are absent. | Seven reviewed UX target files only; not a whole-repository absence claim | OPEN-50; review relevant OPEN-UX decisions; no source pattern approved/rejected here |
| M-04 | Real Progress | 4659 | 4665 | A progress bar shows "Loading…" with no percentage, no step, no ETA. User has no idea if this will take 5 seconds or 5 minutes. | Abandonment on long operations; duplicate submission attempts; support tickets asking "is it stuck?" | For any operation >2 seconds, show **three things simultaneously**: percentage complete, current step name, and estimated time remaining. For operations with defined steps (bulk import, migration, media transcode), show a **stepper** with completed/current/pending states. | Bulk student import, migration engine, media transcode, large exports, institutional reports, contest scoring. | Operations >2s show all three metrics. Step names come from a centralized registry. ETA accuracy tracked post-launch (target ±30%). | PROGRESSIVE_UX_AND_FEEDBACK.md ?1, ?5; DESIGN_SYSTEM.md ?7.7 | conflict | Universal >2s percentage/step/ETA conflicts with measured-only estimates and indeterminate progress. | Seven reviewed UX target files only; not a whole-repository absence claim | OPEN-50; review relevant OPEN-UX decisions; no source pattern approved/rejected here |
| M-05 | Stream It | 4669 | 4675 | User opens the jobs page. One slow query (e.g., company logos) freezes the entire page. The whole screen is blank for 3 seconds, then everything pops in at once. | Perceived load time = slowest request. Users blame the whole page for one slow part. | **Render the shell immediately** (navigation, page header, static chrome), then **fill regions independently** as their data arrives. Use React route-level code splitting and Suspense boundaries. Regions that fail to load show their own error state without affecting neighbors. | Dashboard, profile, job detail, course detail, admin console, institutional analytics. | Every page has a defined shell + ≥2 independently streamed regions. One region failing never blanks the page. TTFB <800ms for the shell. | PROGRESSIVE_UX_AND_FEEDBACK.md ?1, ?7; DESIGN_SYSTEM.md ?12.2 | partial | Two streamed regions per page and shell TTFB <800ms are absent. | Seven reviewed UX target files only; not a whole-repository absence claim | OPEN-50; review relevant OPEN-UX decisions; no source pattern approved/rejected here |
| M-06 | The Forever Spinner | 4679 | 4685 | The spinner spins. And spins. And spins. There is no timeout, no error, no retry. The user closes the tab. | This is the one that loses the customer. No error, no exit — they just leave. Highest-severity UX defect. | **Every async operation needs a terminal state.** After 10 seconds, show "Taking longer than expected…" with a cancel option. After 30 seconds, fail with a specific error, correlation ID, and a retry button. Never leave a spinner spinning indefinitely. | Every async operation in the platform — no exceptions. | Every `useQuery`/`useMutation`/fetch has a timeout. `error_code_catalog` contains a timeout entry. R-003 runbook covers mass-timeout incidents. Zero infinite spinners in staging soak test. | PROGRESSIVE_UX_AND_FEEDBACK.md ?1, ?5, ?7 | conflict | Universal 10s warning/30s failure differs from target 5s status/10s remote timeout/60s billing pending. | Seven reviewed UX target files only; not a whole-repository absence claim | OPEN-50; review relevant OPEN-UX decisions; no source pattern approved/rejected here |
| M-07 | Optimistic Update With Rollback | 4689 | 4695 | User clicks "Like" on a post. The heart fills instantly, then 400ms later it unfills because the server rejected it. No explanation. | User distrust; second-guessing whether their action "took"; abandonment of low-stakes interactions. | Optimistic updates must ship with a **visible rollback path**: on failure, show a small inline error ("Couldn't save. Retry?") *near the element*, not just a global toast. For high-stakes actions (Apply, Purchase, Submit), do NOT use optimistic UI — show explicit loading state and confirmation. | Reactions, follows, bookmarks, saves, mutes, dismissals (optimistic OK). Applications, purchases, submissions (never optimistic). | Optimistic patterns documented per action type. Rollback UX tested with forced 500 response. | PROGRESSIVE_UX_AND_FEEDBACK.md ?6.1; ACTION_REGISTRY.md ACT-NTF-01 | conflict | Source requires nearby inline rollback/retry; target specifies toast or revert without equivalent inline requirement. | Seven reviewed UX target files only; not a whole-repository absence claim | OPEN-50; review relevant OPEN-UX decisions; no source pattern approved/rejected here |
| M-08 | The Cold Start Problem | 4699 | 4705 | First-time user lands on the dashboard. Everything is empty. No guidance, no next step. Just "No data" in six panels. | 40%+ of new users abandon within 2 minutes when the first screen is a void. | First-time users get a **guided empty state** — never a blank dashboard. Show 2–3 clearly labeled actions ("Complete your profile", "Take your first challenge", "Browse open roles"). Track which one they click. Adapt the second session based on their first action. | Dashboard, jobs, courses, applications, portfolio, notifications, feed, institutional admin. | Every empty state has a primary CTA. First-session funnel instrumented (EVT-001 → first meaningful action). Target: 40% complete first action within 24h. | PROGRESSIVE_UX_AND_FEEDBACK.md ?2; NAVIGATION_ARCHITECTURE.md ?8; SCREEN_SPECIFICATIONS_KEY.md ?2 | partial | Guided actions, second-session adaptation, EVT-001 funnel and 40%/24h target absent. | Seven reviewed UX target files only; not a whole-repository absence claim | OPEN-50; review relevant OPEN-UX decisions; no source pattern approved/rejected here |

Only escalate to a full skeleton if cache is >24h old or explicitly invalidated. | Profile, feed, notifications, job list, course list, saved searches. | Repeat navigation within 5 minutes uses cache.
